flag info:
 -d = path to the desired working dir
 -r = path to reads
 -p = <plate_barcode.fasta> file with plate barcodes fasta with path
 -w = <well_barcode.fasta> file with well barcodes fasta with path
 -c = do_porechop: 0 or 1 for whether or not to re-do the porechop step
 -l = min_length: minimum desired length cutoff for filtering reads
